The algorithm accepts an optional `controller`\nobject implementing the ParamController interface to allow dynamic parameter\nadjustment (F, CR).\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom typing import Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple\nimport numpy as np\nimport os\nimport sys\n\n# Import SEED from config if available\n_config_seed = None\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\nfrom config import SEED as _config_seed\n\n\ndef evaluate_problem(problem, x):\n    \"\"\"Evaluate problem, handling both MetaBox (problem.func) and cocoex (problem(x)) styles.\"\"\"\n    if not isinstance(x, np.ndarray):\n        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)\n    \n    if hasattr(problem, 'func'):\n        # MetaBox style: use problem.func(x), which REQUIRES 2D input [NP, dim]\n        if x.ndim == 1:\n            x = x.reshape(1, -1)\n        result = problem.func(x)\n        return float(np.mean(np.array(result).flatten()))\n    else:\n        # cocoex style: problem(x)\n        return float(problem(x))\n\n\nclass DEOptimizer:\n    \"\"\"Differential Evolution optimizer with DQN controller support.\n    \n    The controller outputs F and CR parameters dynamically during optimization.\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, dim: int, lower_bounds, upper_bounds, max_evals: int,\n                 params: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None, controller=None, recorder=None,\n                 swarm_size: int = None):\n        self.dim = int(dim)\n        if lower_bounds is not None:\n            self.lower_bounds = np.asarray(lower_bounds, dtype=float)\n            if self.lower_bounds.ndim == 0:\n                self.lower_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.lower_bounds))\n        else:\n            self.lower_bounds = -5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n        \n        if upper_bounds is not None:\n            self.upper_bounds = np.asarray(upper_bounds, dtype=float)\n            if self.upper_bounds.ndim == 0:\n                self.upper_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.upper_bounds))\n        else:\n            self.upper_bounds = 5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n        \n        self.max_evals = int(max_evals)\n        self.params = dict(params) if params is not None else {}\n        self.controller = controller\n        self.recorder = recorder\n        self.stats = {}\n        self.pop_size = swarm_size if swarm_size is not None else max(10, 5 * self.dim)\n        self.F = float(self.params.get('F', 0.5))\n        self.CR = float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n\n    def _tournament_select(self, fitness, tournsize, rng):\n        \"\"\"Tournament selection with tournsize.\"\"\"\n        selected = []\n        for _ in range(tournsize):\n            idx = int(rng.randint(0, len(fitness)))\n            selected.append(idx)\n        best_idx = selected[0]\n        for idx in selected[1:]:\n            if fitness[idx] < fitness[best_idx]:\n                best_idx = idx\n        return best_idx\n\n    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\n        if self.controller is not None:\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\n\n        pop_size = self.pop_size\n        min_pop_size = max(4, int(0.2 * pop_size))\n        lb = self.lower_bounds\n        ub = self.upper_bounds\n        \n        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\n\n        # Latin Hypercube Sampling initialization for better initial coverage\n        pop = np.empty((pop_size, self.dim))\n        for j in range(self.dim):\n            sample_perm = rng.permutation(pop_size)\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                pop[i, j] = lb[j] + (sample_perm[i] + rng.rand()) / pop_size * (ub[j] - lb[j])\n        \n        # Evaluate initial population\n        evals = 0\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n        \n        for i in range(pop_size):\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                break\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\n            evals += 1\n\n        # Track best\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\n        \n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\n\n        # ---- SHADE (Success-History based Adaptive DE) ----\n        # Memory of successful F and CR values\n        H = 6\n        MF = np.full(H, 0.5)\n        MCR = np.full(H, 0.5)\n        k_mem = 0\n\n        # External archive of replaced individuals\n        archive = np.empty((0, self.dim))\n        archive_cap = max(1, pop_size)\n\n        # Main loop\n        iteration = 0\n        done = False\n        \n        while not done:\n            iteration += 1\n            params = dict(self.params)\n            if self.controller is not None:\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n                    params.update(step_params)\n            \n            base_F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\n            base_CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\n            pbest_ratio = float(params.get('pbest_ratio', 0.2))\n            archive_ratio = float(params.get('archive_ratio', 1.0))\n            \n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n\n            # Sort indices by fitness to support pbest selection\n            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\n            pbest_num = max(1, int(pbest_ratio * pop_size))\n\n            # Per-individual parameters and success records\n            F_vals = np.zeros(pop_size)\n            CR_vals = np.zeros(pop_size)\n            S_F = []\n            S_CR = []\n            S_delta = []\n\n            # Generate trial vectors using current-to-pbest/1 with archive\n            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                # Sample F and CR from SHADE memory distributions\n                mi = int(rng.randint(H))\n                F = float(np.clip(rng.standard_cauchy() * 0.1 + MF[mi], 0.0, 1.0))\n                if not np.isfinite(F) or F <= 0.0:\n                    F = base_F\n                F_vals[i] = float(np.clip(F, 0.05, 1.0))\n\n                CR = float(np.clip(rng.normal(MCR[mi], 0.1), 0.0, 1.0))\n                if not np.isfinite(CR):\n                    CR = base_CR\n                CR_vals[i] = float(np.clip(CR, 0.0, 1.0))\n\n                # Choose pbest (best 20% individuals)\n                pi = sorted_idx[rng.randint(pbest_num)]\n\n                # Choose r1 from population excluding current i\n                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\n                if len(candidates) < 1:\n                    continue\n                r1 = int(rng.choice(candidates))\n\n                # Choose r2 from archive (with probability) or from population\n                use_archive = len(archive) > 0 and rng.rand() < archive_ratio\n                if use_archive:\n                    idx2 = int(rng.randint(len(archive)))\n                    x_r2 = archive[idx2]\n                else:\n                    # ensure r2 distinct from i and r1 when selecting from population\n                    candidates2 = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i and idx != r1]\n                    if len(candidates2) < 1:\n                        continue\n                    r2 = int(rng.choice(candidates2))\n                    x_r2 = pop[r2]\n\n                xi = pop[i]\n                x_pbest = pop[pi]\n                x_r1 = pop[r1]\n\n                # current-to-pbest/1 with optional archive\n                v = xi + F_vals[i] * (x_pbest - xi) + F_vals[i] * (x_r1 - x_r2)\n\n                # Binomial crossover\n                u = np.copy(xi)\n                j_rand = rng.randint(self.dim)\n                for j in range(self.dim):\n                    if rng.rand() < CR_vals[i] or j == j_rand:\n                        u[j] = v[j]\n\n                # Boundary handling: random reinitialization (better than clipping for multimodal problems)\n                for j in range(self.dim):\n                    if u[j] < lb[j] or u[j] > ub[j]:\n                        u[j] = lb[j] + rng.rand() * (ub[j] - lb[j])\n\n                new_pop[i] = u\n\n            # Evaluate trial vectors\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                    done = True\n                    break\n                old_f = float(fitness[i])\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\n                evals += 1\n                \n                # Greedy selection with archive update\n                if trial_f < old_f:\n                    # Add replaced individual to archive\n                    if len(archive) < archive_cap:\n                        archive = np.vstack([archive, pop[i]])\n                    else:\n                        ar = rng.randint(archive_cap)\n                        archive[ar] = pop[i]\n\n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n\n                    # Record successful parameters for memory update\n                    S_F.append(F_vals[i])\n                    S_CR.append(CR_vals[i])\n                    S_delta.append(max(1e-30, abs(old_f - trial_f)))\n\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\n\n            # Update SHADE success-history memories\n            if len(S_F) > 0:\n                s_f = np.asarray(S_F)\n                s_cr = np.asarray(S_CR)\n                w = np.asarray(S_delta, dtype=float)\n                w = w / np.sum(w)\n\n                # Weighted Lehmer mean for F\n                num = float(np.sum(w * s_f * s_f))\n                den = float(np.sum(w * s_f))\n                if den > 1e-12:\n                    MF[k_mem] = num / den\n                else:\n                    MF[k_mem] = 0.5\n\n                # Weighted arithmetic mean for CR\n                MCR[k_mem] = float(np.sum(w * s_cr))\n\n                k_mem = (k_mem + 1) % H\n\n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n            \n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\n            \n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n\n        self.stats = {\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\n            'evals': int(evals),\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\n        }\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\"\n    }\n    </json>",

---- SHADE (Success-History based Adaptive DE) ----\n   136\t        # Memory of successful F and CR values\n   137\t        H = 6\n   138\t        MF = np.full(H, 0.5)\n   139\t        MCR = np.full(H, 0.5)\n   140\t        k_mem = 0\n   141\t\n   142\t        # External archive of replaced individuals\n   143\t        archive = np.empty((0, self.dim))\n   144\t        archive_cap = max(1, pop_size)\n   145\t\n   146\t        # Main loop\n   147\t        iteration = 0\n   148\t        done = False\n   149\t        \n   150\t        while not done:\n   151\t            iteration += 1\n   152\t            params = dict(self.params)\n   153\t            if self.controller is not None:\n   154\t                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n   155\t                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n   156\t                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n   157\t                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n   158\t                    params.update(step_params)\n   159\t            \n   160\t            base_F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\n   161\t            base_CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\n   162\t            pbest_ratio = float(params.get('pbest_ratio', 0.2))\n   163\t            archive_ratio = float(params.get('archive_ratio', 1.0))\n   164\t            \n   165\t            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n   166\t\n   167\t            # Sort indices by fitness to support pbest selection\n   168\t            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\n   169\t            pbest_num = max(1, int(pbest_ratio * pop_size))\n   170\t\n   171\t            # Per-individual parameters and success records\n   172\t            F_vals = np.zeros(pop_size)\n   173\t            CR_vals = np.zeros(pop_size)\n   174\t            S_F = []\n   175\t            S_CR = []\n   176\t            S_delta = []\n   177\t\n   178\t            # Generate trial vectors using current-to-pbest/1 with archive\n   179\t            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\n   180\t            for i in range(pop_size):\n   181\t                # Sample F and CR from SHADE memory distributions\n   182\t                mi = int(rng.randint(H))\n   183\t                F = float(np.clip(rng.standard_cauchy() * 0.1 + MF[mi], 0.0, 1.0))\n   184\t                if not np.isfinite(F) or F <= 0.0:\n   185\t                    F = base_F\n   186\t                F_vals[i] = float(np.clip(F, 0.05, 1.0))\n   187\t\n   188\t                CR = float(np.clip(rng.normal(MCR[mi], 0.1), 0.0, 1.0))\n   189\t                if not np.isfinite(CR):\n   190\t                    CR = base_CR\n   191\t                CR_vals[i] = float(np.clip(CR, 0.0, 1.0))\n   192\t\n   193\t                # Choose pbest (best 20% individuals)\n   194\t                pi = sorted_idx[rng.randint(pbest_num)]\n   195\t\n   196\t                # Choose r1 from population excluding current i\n   197\t                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\n   198\t                if len(candidates) < 1:\n   199\t                    continue\n   200\t                r1 = int(rng.choice(candidates))\n   201\t\n   202\t                # Choose r2 from archive (with probability) or from population\n   203\t                use_archive = len(archive) > 0 and rng.rand() < archive_ratio\n   204\t                if use_archive:\n   205\t                    idx2 = int(rng.randint(len(archive)))\n   206\t                    x_r2 = archive[idx2]\n   207\t                else:\n   208\t                    # ensure r2 distinct from i and r1 when selecting from population\n   209\t                    candidates2 = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i and idx != r1]\n   210\t                    if len(candidates2) < 1:\n   211\t                        continue\n   212\t                    r2 = int(rng.choice(candidates2))\n   213\t                    x_r2 = pop[r2]\n   214\t\n   215\t                xi = pop[i]\n   216\t                x_pbest = pop[pi]\n   217\t                x_r1 = pop[r1]\n   218\t\n   219\t                # current-to-pbest/1 with optional archive\n   220\t                v = xi + F_vals[i] * (x_pbest - xi) + F_vals[i] * (x_r1 - x_r2)\n   221\t\n   222\t                # Binomial crossover\n   223\t                u = np.copy(xi)\n   224\t                j_rand = rng.randint(self.dim)\n   225\t                for j in range(self.dim):\n   226\t                    if rng.rand() < CR_vals[i] or j == j_rand:\n   227\t                        u[j] = v[j]\n   228\t\n   229\t                # Boundary handling: random reinitialization (better than clipping for multimodal problems)\n   230\t                for j in range(self.dim):\n   231\t                    if u[j] < lb[j] or u[j] > ub[j]:\n   232\t                        u[j] = lb[j] + rng.rand() * (ub[j] - lb[j])\n   233\t\n   234\t                new_pop[i] = u\n   235\t\n   236\t            # Evaluate trial vectors\n   237\t            for i in range(pop_size):\n   238\t                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n   239\t                    done = True\n   240\t                    break\n   241\t                old_f = float(fitness[i])\n   242\t                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\n   243\t                evals += 1\n   244\t                \n   245\t                # Greedy selection with archive update\n   246\t                if trial_f < old_f:\n   247\t                    # Add replaced individual to archive\n   248\t                    if len(archive) < archive_cap:\n   249\t                        archive = np.vstack([archive, pop[i]])\n   250\t                    else:\n   251\t                        ar = rng.randint(archive_cap)\n   252\t                        archive[ar] = pop[i]\n   253\t\n   254\t                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\n   255\t                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n   256\t\n   257\t                    # Record successful parameters for memory update\n   258\t                    S_F.append(F_vals[i])\n   259\t                    S_CR.append(CR_vals[i])\n   260\t                    S_delta.append(max(1e-30, abs(old_f - trial_f)))\n   261\t\n   262\t                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n   263\t                        gbest_f = trial_f\n   264\t                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\n   265\t\n   266\t            # Update SHADE success-history memories\n   267\t            if len(S_F) > 0:\n   268\t                s_f = np.asarray(S_F)\n   269\t                s_cr = np.asarray(S_CR)\n   270\t                w = np.asarray(S_delta, dtype=float)\n   271\t                w = w / np.sum(w)\n   272\t\n   273\t                # Weighted Lehmer mean for F\n   274\t                num = float(np.sum(w * s_f * s_f))\n   275\t                den = float(np.sum(w * s_f))\n   276\t                if den > 1e-12:\n   277\t                    MF[k_mem] = num / den\n   278\t                else:\n   279\t                    MF[k_mem] = 0.5\n   280\t\n   281\t                # Weighted arithmetic mean for CR\n   282\t                MCR[k_mem] = float(np.sum(w * s_cr))\n   283\t\n   284\t                k_mem = (k_mem + 1) % H\n   285\t\n   286\t            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n   287\t            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n   288\t            \n   289\t            # It can load/save a model\n     6\tand be used at inference time inside the DE optimizer.\n     7\t\"\"\"\n     8\tfrom typing import Dict, Any\n     9\timport os\n    10\timport numpy as np\n    11\ttry:\n    12\t    import torch\n    13\t    import torch.nn as nn\n    14\t    import torch.nn.functional as F\n    15\texcept Exception:\n    16\t    torch = None\n    17\t\n    18\tfrom param_controller import ParamController\n    19\t\n    20\timport sys\n    21\tsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\n    22\tfrom config import SEED, set_global_seed\n    23\t\n    24\tif torch is not None:\n    25\t    class _Net(nn.Module):\n    26\t        def __init__(self, obs_dim, action_dim, seed=None):\n    27\t            super().__init__()\n    28\t            self.fc1 = nn.Linear(obs_dim, 128)\n    29\t            self.fc2 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n    30\t            self.fc3 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n    31\t            self.out = nn.Linear(128, action_dim)\n    32\t            \n    33\t            # \u5982\u679c\u63d0\u4f9b\u4e86\u79cd\u5b50\uff0c\u4f7f\u7528\u5b83\u6765\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u6743\u91cd\n    34\t            if seed is not None:\n    35\t                self._init_weights(seed)\n    36\t\n    37\t        def _init_weights(self, seed):\n    38\t            \"\"\"\u4f7f\u7528\u56fa\u5b9a\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u7f51\u7edc\u6743\u91cd\"\"\"\n    39\t            g = torch.Generator()\n    40\t            g.manual_seed(seed)\n    41\t            \n    42\t            # \u4f7f\u7528Kaiming\u521d\u59cb\u5316\n    43\t            for m in [self.fc1, self.fc2, self.fc3, self.out]:\n    44\t                torch.nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(m.weight, nonlinearity='leaky_relu')\n    45\t                torch.nn.init.zeros_(m.bias)\n    46\t\n    47\t        def forward(self, x):\n    48\t            x = x.type_as(self.fc1.weight)\n    49\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc1(x), 0.2)\n    50\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc2(x), 0.2)\n    51\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc3(x), 0.2)\n    52\t            return self.out(x)\n    53\t\n    54\t\n    55\tclass DQNController(ParamController):\n    56\t    \"\"\"Simple continuous-action DQN controller for DE parameters.\n    57\t\n    58\t    The network outputs unbounded real values; `step` maps them to\n    59\t    DE parameter ranges: F in [0.1, 1.0], CR in [0.0, 1.0].\n    60\t    \"\"\"\n    61\t    def __init__(self, model_path: str = None, obs_dim: int = 3, device: str = 'cpu'):\n    62\t        if torch is None:\n    63\t            raise RuntimeError('torch is required for DQNController')\n    64\t        self.device = torch.device(device)\n    65\t        self.obs_dim = obs_dim\n    66\t        self.action_dim = 2  # F, CR\n    67\t        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.action_dim).to(self.device)\n    68\t        if model_path and os.path.exists(model_path):\n    69\t            self.load(model_path)\n    70\t\n    71\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    72\t        return None\n    73\t\n    74\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    75\t        it = float(observation.get('iteration', 0))\n    76\t        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\n    77\t        gbest_f = float(observation.get('gbest_f', 0.0))\n    78\t        init_f = float(observation.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f))\n    79\t        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', max(1.0, evals)))\n    80\t        # normalized progress and relative improvement\n    81\t        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n    82\t        if init_f != 0.0 and gbest_f != 0.0 and np.isfinite(init_f) and np.isfinite(gbest_f):\n    83\t            rel_improve = max(0.0, min(1.0, (np.log(abs(init_f)) - np.log(abs(gbest_f))) / max(1.0, abs(np.log(abs(init_f))))))\n    84\t        else:\n    85\t            rel_improve = 0.0\n    86\t        obs = [progress, rel_improve, gbest_f]\n    87\t        \n    88\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n    89\t        x = torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device).unsqueeze(0)\n    90\t        \n    91\t        with torch.no_grad():\n    92\t            out_t = self.net(x).squeeze(0).cpu()\n    93\t            try:\n    94\t                out_t = torch.nan_to_num(out_t, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    95\t            except Exception:\n    96\t                out_np = out_t.numpy()\n    97\t                out_np = np.nan_to_num(out_np, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    98\t                out_t = torch.from_numpy(out_np)\n    99\t            out_t = torch.clamp(out_t, -10.0, 10.0)\n   100\t\n   101\t        out = out_t.numpy()\n   102\t\n   103\t        # Map F (differential weight) with sigmoid to [0.1, 1.0]\n   104\t        try:\n   105\t            f_raw = float(out[0])\n   106\t            f_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-f_raw)))\n   107\t        except Exception:\n   108\t            f_sig = 0.5\n   109\t        F = 0.1 + (1.0 - 0.1) * f_sig\n   110\t        \n   111\t        # Map CR (crossover probability) with sigmoid to [0.0, 1.0]\n   112\t        try:\n   113\t            cr_raw = float(out[1])\n   114\t            cr_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-cr_raw)))\n   115\t        except Exception:\n   116\t            cr_sig = 0.9\n   117\t        CR = 0.0 + (1.0 - 0.0) * cr_sig\n   118\t        \n   119\t        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR)}\n   120\t\n   121\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   122\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   123\t\n   124\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   125\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n   126\t\n   127\t\n   128\tclass DiscreteDQNController(ParamController):\n   129\t    \"\"\"Discrete-action DQN controller for DE parameters with online training.\n   130\t\n   131\t    Discretizes F and CR into `bins` equally spaced values and\n   132\t    treats the Cartesian product as the discrete action space.\n   133\t    The network outpu\n   134\t<response clipped>\n   135\trm]\n   136\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n   137\t        return torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device)\n   138\t\n   139\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n   140\t        x = self._obs_to_tensor(observation)\n   141\t\n   142\t        # Epsilon-greedy action selection (only in training mode)\n   143\t        if self.training and self.rng.random() < self.epsilon:\n   144\t            idx = int(self.rng.randint(self.n_actions))\n   145\t        else:\n   146\t            with torch.no_grad():\n   147\t                q = self.net(x.unsqueeze(0)).squeeze(0).cpu().numpy()\n   148\t            idx = int(np.argmax(q))\n   149\t\n   150\t        if self.training:\n   151\t            # Store transition placeholder; reward will be set later via store_reward()\n   152\t            self._last_obs = observation\n   153\t            self._last_action_idx = idx\n   154\t            self._last_obs_tensor = x\n   155\t\n   156\t        return dict(self.actions[idx])\n   157\t\n   158\t    def store_reward(self, reward: float, next_observation: Dict[str, Any], done: bool):\n   159\t        \"\"\"Store a complete transition (obs, action, reward, next_obs, done) in the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   160\t        # Always track total reward (for both train and test)\n   161\t        self.total_reward += reward\n   162\t        self.current_sweep_reward += reward\n   163\t        \n   164\t        if not self.training:\n   165\t            return\n   166\t\n   167\t        next_obs_tensor = self._obs_to_tensor(next_observation)\n   168\t        transition = (\n   169\t            self._last_obs_tensor.clone(),\n   170\t            self._last_action_idx,\n   171\t            reward,\n   172\t            next_obs_tensor.clone(),\n   173\t            done\n   174\t        )\n   175\t\n   176\t        if len(self.replay_buffer) < self.buffer_capacity:\n   177\t            self.replay_buffer.append(transition)\n   178\t        else:\n   179\t            self.replay_buffer[self.buffer_pos] = transition\n   180\t            self.buffer_pos = (self.buffer_pos + 1) % self.buffer_capacity\n   181\t\n   182\t        self.step_count += 1\n   183\t\n   184\t        # Record return every N updates and reset counter\n   185\t        if self.step_count % self.return_record_interval == 0:\n   186\t            self.reward_history.append(self.current_sweep_reward)\n   187\t            self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   188\t\n   189\t        # Train every update_freq steps\n   190\t        if self.step_count % self.update_freq == 0 and len(self.replay_buffer) >= self.batch_size:\n   191\t            loss = self._train_step()\n   192\t            self.total_loss += loss\n   193\t            self.loss_history.append(loss)\n   194\t\n   195\t    def _train_step(self) -> float:\n   196\t        \"\"\"Perform one DQN training step on a batch from the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   197\t        batch = self.rng.choice(len(self.replay_buffer), self.batch_size, replace=False).tolist()\n   198\t        batch = [self.replay_buffer[i] for i in batch]\n   199\t\n   200\t        obs_b = torch.stack([t[0] for t in batch])\n   201\t        act_b = torch.tensor([t[1] for t in batch], dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)\n   202\t        rew_b = torch.tensor([t[2] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   203\t        next_b = torch.stack([t[3] for t in batch])\n   204\t        done_b = torch.tensor([t[4] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   205\t\n   206\t        q_values = self.net(obs_b)\n   207\t        q_sa = q_values.gather(1, act_b.unsqueeze(1)).squeeze(1)\n   208\t\n   209\t        with torch.no_grad():\n   210\t            q_next = self.target_net(next_b)\n   211\t            q_next_max = q_next.max(1)[0]\n   212\t            target = rew_b + self.gamma * (1.0 - done_b) * q_next_max\n   213\t\n   214\t        loss = F.mse_loss(q_sa, target)\n   215\t        self.optimizer.zero_grad()\n   216\t        loss.backward()\n   217\t        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(self.net.parameters(), self.max_grad_norm)\n   218\t        self.optimizer.step()\n   219\t\n   220\t        # Update target network periodically\n   221\t        if self.step_count % self.target_update_freq == 0:\n   222\t            self.target_net.load_state_dict(self.net.state_dict())\n   223\t            # Decay epsilon\n   224\t            self.epsilon = max(self.epsilon_end, self.epsilon * self.epsilon_decay)\n   225\t\n   226\t        return loss.item()\n   227\t\n   228\t    def reset_loss(self):\n   229\t        \"\"\"Reset loss and reward counters for a new sweep.\"\"\"\n   230\t        self.total_loss = 0.0\n   231\t        self.total_reward = 0.0\n   232\t        self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   233\t        self.reward_history = []\n   234\t\n   235\t    def get_loss_history(self) -> list:\n   236\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded losses.\"\"\"\n   237\t        return self.loss_history\n   238\t\n   239\t    def get_reward_history(self) -> list:\n   240\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded returns (every N updates).\"\"\"\n   241\t        return self.reward_history\n   242\t\n   243\t    def get_total_loss(self) -> float:\n   244\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative training loss.\"\"\"\n   245\t        return self.total_loss\n   246\t\n   247\t    def get_total_reward(self) -> float:\n   248\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative reward from environment interactions.\"\"\"\n   249\t        return self.total_reward\n   250\t\n   251\t    def action_to_index(self, action: Dict[str, float]) -> int:\n   252\t        a = np.array([action.get('F', 0.8), action.get('CR', 0.9)], dtype=float)\n   253\t        acts = np.array([[aa['F'], aa['CR']] for aa in self.actions], dtype=float)\n   254\t        d = np.sum((acts - a[None, :])**2, axis=1)\n   255\t        return int(np.argmin(d))\n   256\t\n   257\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   258\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   259\t\n   260\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   261\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n\"\n    }\n    </json>",

It can load/save a model\n     6\tand be used at inference time inside the DE optimizer.\n     7\t\"\"\"\n     8\tfrom typing import Dict, Any\n     9\timport os\n    10\timport numpy as np\n    11\ttry:\n    12\t    import torch\n    13\t    import torch.nn as nn\n    14\t    import torch.nn.functional as F\n    15\texcept Exception:\n    16\t    torch = None\n    17\t\n    18\tfrom param_controller import ParamController\n    19\t\n    20\timport sys\n    21\tsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\n    22\tfrom config import SEED, set_global_seed\n    23\t\n    24\tif torch is not None:\n    25\t    class _Net(nn.Module):\n    26\t        def __init__(self, obs_dim, action_dim, seed=None):\n    27\t            super().__init__()\n    28\t            self.fc1 = nn.Linear(obs_dim, 128)\n    29\t            self.fc2 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n    30\t            self.fc3 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n    31\t            self.out = nn.Linear(128, action_dim)\n    32\t            \n    33\t            # \u4f7f\u7528Kaiming\u521d\u59cb\u5316\n    43\t            for m in [self.fc1, self.fc2, self.fc3, self.out]:\n    44\t                torch.nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(m.weight, nonlinearity='leaky_relu')\n    45\t                torch.nn.init.zeros_(m.bias)\n    46\t\n    47\t        def forward(self, x):\n    48\t            x = x.type_as(self.fc1.weight)\n    49\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc1(x), 0.2)\n    50\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc2(x), 0.2)\n    51\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc3(x), 0.2)\n    52\t            return self.out(x)\n    53\t\n    54\t\n    55\tclass DQNController(ParamController):\n    56\t    \"\"\"Simple continuous-action DQN controller for DE parameters.\n    57\t\n    58\t    The network outputs unbounded real values; `step` maps them to\n    59\t    DE parameter ranges: F in [0.1, 1.0], CR in [0.0, 1.0].\n    60\t    \"\"\"\n    61\t    def __init__(self, model_path: str = None, obs_dim: int = 3, device: str = 'cpu'):\n    62\t        if torch is None:\n    63\t            raise RuntimeError('torch is required for DQNController')\n    64\t        self.device = torch.device(device)\n    65\t        self.obs_dim = obs_dim\n    66\t        self.action_dim = 2  # F, CR\n    67\t        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.action_dim).to(self.device)\n    68\t        if model_path and os.path.exists(model_path):\n    69\t            self.load(model_path)\n    70\t\n    71\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    72\t        return None\n    73\t\n    74\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    75\t        it = float(observation.get('iteration', 0))\n    76\t        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\n    77\t        gbest_f = float(observation.get('gbest_f', 0.0))\n    78\t        init_f = float(observation.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f))\n    79\t        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', max(1.0, evals)))\n    80\t        # normalized progress and relative improvement\n    81\t        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n    82\t        if init_f != 0.0 and gbest_f != 0.0 and np.isfinite(init_f) and np.isfinite(gbest_f):\n    83\t            rel_improve = max(0.0, min(1.0, (np.log(abs(init_f)) - np.log(abs(gbest_f))) / max(1.0, abs(np.log(abs(init_f))))))\n    84\t        else:\n    85\t            rel_improve = 0.0\n    86\t        obs = [progress, rel_improve, gbest_f]\n    87\t        \n    88\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n    89\t        x = torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device).unsqueeze(0)\n    90\t        \n    91\t        with torch.no_grad():\n    92\t            out_t = self.net(x).squeeze(0).cpu()\n    93\t            try:\n    94\t                out_t = torch.nan_to_num(out_t, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    95\t            except Exception:\n    96\t                out_np = out_t.numpy()\n    97\t                out_np = np.nan_to_num(out_np, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    98\t                out_t = torch.from_numpy(out_np)\n    99\t            out_t = torch.clamp(out_t, -10.0, 10.0)\n   100\t\n   101\t        out = out_t.numpy()\n   102\t\n   103\t        # Map F (differential weight) with sigmoid to [0.1, 1.0]\n   104\t        try:\n   105\t            f_raw = float(out[0])\n   106\t            f_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-f_raw)))\n   107\t        except Exception:\n   108\t            f_sig = 0.5\n   109\t        F = 0.1 + (1.0 - 0.1) * f_sig\n   110\t        \n   111\t        # Map CR (crossover probability) with sigmoid to [0.0, 1.0]\n   112\t        try:\n   113\t            cr_raw = float(out[1])\n   114\t            cr_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-cr_raw)))\n   115\t        except Exception:\n   116\t            cr_sig = 0.9\n   117\t        CR = 0.0 + (1.0 - 0.0) * cr_sig\n   118\t        \n   119\t        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR)}\n   120\t\n   121\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   122\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   123\t\n   124\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   125\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n   126\t\n   127\t\n   128\tclass DiscreteDQNController(ParamController):\n   129\t    \"\"\"Discrete-action DQN controller for DE parameters with online training.\n   130\t\n   131\t    Discretizes F and CR into `bins` equally spaced values and\n   132\t    treats the Cartesian product as the discrete action space.\n   133\t    The network outpu\n   134\t<response clipped>\n   135\trm]\n   136\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n   137\t        return torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device)\n   138\t\n   139\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n   140\t        x = self._obs_to_tensor(observation)\n   141\t\n   142\t        # Epsilon-greedy action selection (only in training mode)\n   143\t        if self.training and self.rng.random() < self.epsilon:\n   144\t            idx = int(self.rng.randint(self.n_actions))\n   145\t        else:\n   146\t            with torch.no_grad():\n   147\t                q = self.net(x.unsqueeze(0)).squeeze(0).cpu().numpy()\n   148\t            idx = int(np.argmax(q))\n   149\t\n   150\t        if self.training:\n   151\t            # Store transition placeholder; reward will be set later via store_reward()\n   152\t            self._last_obs = observation\n   153\t            self._last_action_idx = idx\n   154\t            self._last_obs_tensor = x\n   155\t\n   156\t        return dict(self.actions[idx])\n   157\t\n   158\t    def store_reward(self, reward: float, next_observation: Dict[str, Any], done: bool):\n   159\t        \"\"\"Store a complete transition (obs, action, reward, next_obs, done) in the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   160\t        # Always track total reward (for both train and test)\n   161\t        self.total_reward += reward\n   162\t        self.current_sweep_reward += reward\n   163\t        \n   164\t        if not self.training:\n   165\t            return\n   166\t\n   167\t        next_obs_tensor = self._obs_to_tensor(next_observation)\n   168\t        transition = (\n   169\t            self._last_obs_tensor.clone(),\n   170\t            self._last_action_idx,\n   171\t            reward,\n   172\t            next_obs_tensor.clone(),\n   173\t            done\n   174\t        )\n   175\t\n   176\t        if len(self.replay_buffer) < self.buffer_capacity:\n   177\t            self.replay_buffer.append(transition)\n   178\t        else:\n   179\t            self.replay_buffer[self.buffer_pos] = transition\n   180\t            self.buffer_pos = (self.buffer_pos + 1) % self.buffer_capacity\n   181\t\n   182\t        self.step_count += 1\n   183\t\n   184\t        # Record return every N updates and reset counter\n   185\t        if self.step_count % self.return_record_interval == 0:\n   186\t            self.reward_history.append(self.current_sweep_reward)\n   187\t            self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   188\t\n   189\t        # Train every update_freq steps\n   190\t        if self.step_count % self.update_freq == 0 and len(self.replay_buffer) >= self.batch_size:\n   191\t            loss = self._train_step()\n   192\t            self.total_loss += loss\n   193\t            self.loss_history.append(loss)\n   194\t\n   195\t    def _train_step(self) -> float:\n   196\t        \"\"\"Perform one DQN training step on a batch from the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   197\t        batch = self.rng.choice(len(self.replay_buffer), self.batch_size, replace=False).tolist()\n   198\t        batch = [self.replay_buffer[i] for i in batch]\n   199\t\n   200\t        obs_b = torch.stack([t[0] for t in batch])\n   201\t        act_b = torch.tensor([t[1] for t in batch], dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)\n   202\t        rew_b = torch.tensor([t[2] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   203\t        next_b = torch.stack([t[3] for t in batch])\n   204\t        done_b = torch.tensor([t[4] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   205\t\n   206\t        q_values = self.net(obs_b)\n   207\t        q_sa = q_values.gather(1, act_b.unsqueeze(1)).squeeze(1)\n   208\t\n   209\t        with torch.no_grad():\n   210\t            q_next = self.target_net(next_b)\n   211\t            q_next_max = q_next.max(1)[0]\n   212\t            target = rew_b + self.gamma * (1.0 - done_b) * q_next_max\n   213\t\n   214\t        loss = F.mse_loss(q_sa, target)\n   215\t        self.optimizer.zero_grad()\n   216\t        loss.backward()\n   217\t        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(self.net.parameters(), self.max_grad_norm)\n   218\t        self.optimizer.step()\n   219\t\n   220\t        # Update target network periodically\n   221\t        if self.step_count % self.target_update_freq == 0:\n   222\t            self.target_net.load_state_dict(self.net.state_dict())\n   223\t            # Decay epsilon\n   224\t            self.epsilon = max(self.epsilon_end, self.epsilon * self.epsilon_decay)\n   225\t\n   226\t        return loss.item()\n   227\t\n   228\t    def reset_loss(self):\n   229\t        \"\"\"Reset loss and reward counters for a new sweep.\"\"\"\n   230\t        self.total_loss = 0.0\n   231\t        self.total_reward = 0.0\n   232\t        self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   233\t        self.reward_history = []\n   234\t\n   235\t    def get_loss_history(self) -> list:\n   236\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded losses.\"\"\"\n   237\t        return self.loss_history\n   238\t\n   239\t    def get_reward_history(self) -> list:\n   240\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded returns (every N updates).\"\"\"\n   241\t        return self.reward_history\n   242\t\n   243\t    def get_total_loss(self) -> float:\n   244\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative training loss.\"\"\"\n   245\t        return self.total_loss\n   246\t\n   247\t    def get_total_reward(self) -> float:\n   248\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative reward from environment interactions.\"\"\"\n   249\t        return self.total_reward\n   250\t\n   251\t    def action_to_index(self, action: Dict[str, float]) -> int:\n   252\t        a = np.array([action.get('F', 0.8), action.get('CR', 0.9)], dtype=float)\n   253\t        acts = np.array([[aa['F'], aa['CR']] for aa in self.actions], dtype=float)\n   254\t        d = np.sum((acts - a[None, :])**2, axis=1)\n   255\t        return int(np.argmin(d))\n   256\t\n   257\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   258\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   259\t\n   260\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   261\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n\"\n    }\n    </json>"

upper_bounds is not None:\n    53\t            self.upper_bounds = np.asarray(upper_bounds, dtype=float)\n    54\t            if self.upper_bounds.ndim == 0:\n    55\t                self.upper_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.upper_bounds))\n    56\t        else:\n    57\t            self.upper_bounds = 5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n    58\t        \n    59\t        self.max_evals = int(max_evals)\n    60\t        self.params = dict(params) if params is not None else {}\n    61\t        self.controller = controller\n    62\t        self.recorder = recorder\n    63\t        self.stats = {}\n    64\t        self.pop_size = swarm_size if swarm_size is not None else max(10, 5 * self.dim)\n    65\t        self.F = float(self.params.get('F', 0.5))\n    66\t        self.CR = float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n    67\t\n    68\t    def _tournament_select(self, fitness, tournsize, rng):\n    69\t        \"\"\"Tournament selection with tournsize.\"\"\"\n    70\t        selected = []\n    71\t        for _ in range(tournsize):\n    72\t            idx = int(rng.randint(0, len(fitness)))\n    73\t            selected.append(idx)\n    74\t        best_idx = selected[0]\n    75\t        for idx in selected[1:]:\n    76\t            if fitness[idx] < fitness[best_idx]:\n    77\t                best_idx = idx\n    78\t        return best_idx\n    79\t\n    80\t    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\n    81\t        if self.controller is not None:\n    82\t            self.controller.reset({'dim': pop_size))\n    86\t        lb = self.lower_bounds\n    87\t        ub = self.upper_bounds\n    88\t        \n    89\t        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\n    90\t        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\n    91\t\n    92\t        # Latin Hypercube Sampling initialization for better initial coverage\n    93\t        initial_pop_size = pop_size\n    94\t 101\t        # Evaluate initial population\n   102\t        evals = 0\n   103\t        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n   104\t        \n   105\t        for i in range(pop_size):\n   106\t Opposition-based learning: evaluate mirrored points and keep the best N\n   112\t        if evals + pop_size <= self.max_evals:\n   113\t            opp_pop = lb + ub - pop\n   114\t            opp_fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n   115\t            for i in range(pop_size):\n   116\t                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n   117\t                    break\n   118\t                opp_fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, opp_pop[i])\n   119\t                evals += 1\n   120\t            all_pop = np.vstack([pop, opp_pop])\n   121\t            all_fit = all_fit[keep_order].copy()\n   125\t\n   126\t        # Track best\n   127\t        best_idx = int\n   128\t<response clipped>\n   129\tidates))\n   130\t\n   131\t                # Choose r2 from archive (with probability) or from population\n   132\t                use_archive = len(archive) > 0 and rng.rand() < archive_ratio\n   133\t                if use_archive:\n   134\t from population\n   138\t                    candidates2 = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i and idx != r1]\n   139\t                    if len(candidates2) < 1:\n   140\t                        continue\n   141\t                    r2 = int(rng.choice(candidates2))\n   142\t                    x_r2 = pop[r2]\n   143\t\n   144\t                xi = pop[i]\n   145\t                x_pbest = pop[pi]\n   146\t                x_r1 = pop[r1]\n   147\t\n   148\t                # current-to-pbest/1 with optional archive\n   149\t                v = xi + F_vals[i] * (x_pbest - xi) + F_vals[i] in range(self.dim):\n   155\t                    if rng.rand() < CR_vals[i] or j == j_rand:\n   156\t                        u[j] = v[j]\n   157\t\n   158\t                # Boundary handling: random reinitialization (better than clipping for multimodal problems)\n   159\t                for j in range(self.dim):\n   160\t                    if u[j] < lb[j] or u[j] > ub[j]:\n   161\t range(pop_size):\n   167\t                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n   168\t                    done = True\n   169\t                    break\n   170\t                old_f = float(fitness[i])\n   171\t                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\n   172\t                evals += 1\n   173\t                \n   174\t                # Greedy selection with archive update\n   175\t                if trial_f < old_f:\n   176\t                    # Add replaced individual to archive\n   177\t                    if len(archive) < archive_cap:\n   178\t                        archive = np.vstack([archive, pop[i]])\n   179\t                    else:\n   180\t                        ar = rng.randint(archive_cap)\n   181\t                        archive[ar] = pop[i]\n   182\t\n   183\t                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\n   184\t                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n   185\t\n   186\t                    # Record successful parameters for memory update\n   187\t                    S_F.append(F_vals[i])\n   188\t                    S_CR.append(CR_vals[i])\n   189\t                    S_delta.append(max(1e-30, abs(old_f - trial_f)))\n   190\t\n   191\t                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n   192\t                        gbest_f = trial_f\n   193\t                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\n   194\t\n   195\t            # Update SHADE success-history memories\n   196\t            if len(S_F) > 0:\n   197\t                s_f = np.asarray(S_F)\n   198\t                s_cr = np.asarray(S_CR)\n   199\t                w = np.asarray(S_delta, dtype=float)\n   200\t                w = w / np.sum(w)\n   201\t\n   202\t                # Weighted Lehmer mean for F\n   203\t                num = float(np.sum(w * s_f * s_f))\n   204\t                den = float(np.sum(w * s_f))\n   205\t                if den > 1e-12:\n   206\t                    MF[k_mem] = num / den\n   207\t                else:\n   208\t                    MF[k_mem] = 0.5\n   209\t\n   210\t                # Weighted arithmetic mean for CR\n   211\t                MCR[k_mem] = float(np.sum(w * s_cr))\n   212\t\n   213\t                k_mem = (k_mem + 1) % H\n   214\t\n   215\t            # L-SHADE: Linear Population Size Reduction (move from exploration to exploitation)\n   216\t            if pop_size > min_pop_size:\n   217\t                new_pop_size = max(min_pop_size, int(round(initial_pop_size + (min_pop_size - initial_pop_size) * (evals / max(1, self.max_evals)))))\n   218\t                if new_pop_size < pop_size:\n   219\t                    keep_order = np.argsort(fitness)[:new_pop_size]\n   220\t                    pop = pop[keep_order].copy()\n   221\t                    fitness = fitness[keep_order].copy()\n   222\t                    pop_size = new_pop_size\n   223\t                    archive_cap = max(1, pop_size)\n   224\t\n   225\t            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n   226\t            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n   227\t            \n   228\t            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\n   229\t            if self.controller is not None and train:\n   230\t                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n   231\t                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n   232\t                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n   233\t                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n   234\t                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\n   235\t            \n   236\t            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n   237\t\n   238\t        self.stats = {\n   239\t The algorithm accepts an optional `controller`\nobject implementing the ParamController interface to allow dynamic parameter\nadjustment (F, CR).\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom typing import Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple\nimport numpy as np\nimport os\nimport sys\n\n# Import SEED from config if available\n_config_seed = None\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\nfrom config import SEED as _config_seed\n\n\ndef evaluate_problem(problem, x):\n    \"\"\"Evaluate problem, handling both MetaBox (problem.func) and cocoex (problem(x)) styles.\"\"\"\n    if not isinstance(x, np.ndarray):\n        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)\n    \n    if hasattr(problem, 'func'):\n        # MetaBox style: use problem.func(x), which REQUIRES 2D input [NP, dim]\n        if x.ndim == 1:\n            x = x.reshape(1, -1)\n        result = problem.func(x)\n        return float(np.mean(np.array(result).flatten()))\n    else:\n        # cocoex style: problem(x)\n        return float(problem(x))\n\n\nclass DEOptimizer:\n    \"\"\"Differential Evolution optimizer with DQN controller support.\n    \n    The controller outputs F and CR parameters dynamically during optimization.\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, dim: int, lower_bounds, upper_bounds, max_evals: int,\n                 params: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None, controller=None, recorder=None,\n                 swarm_size: int = None):\n        self.dim = int(dim)\n        if lower_bounds is not None:\n            self.lower_bounds = np.asarray(lower_bounds, dtype=float)\n            if self.lower_bounds.ndim == 0:\n                self.lower_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.lower_bounds))\n        else:\n            self.lower_bounds = -5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n        \n        if upper_bounds is not None:\n            self.upper_bounds = np.asarray(upper_bounds, dtype=float)\n            if self.upper_bounds.ndim == 0:\n                self.upper_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.upper_bounds))\n        else:\n            self.upper_bounds = 5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n        \n        self.max_evals = int(max_evals)\n        self.params = dict(params) if params is not None else {}\n        self.controller = controller\n        self.recorder = recorder\n        self.stats = {}\n        self.pop_size = swarm_size if swarm_size is not None else max(10, 5 * self.dim)\n        self.F = float(self.params.get('F', 0.5))\n        self.CR = float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n\n    def _tournament_select(self, fitness, tournsize, rng):\n        \"\"\"Tournament selection with tournsize.\"\"\"\n        selected = []\n        for _ in range(tournsize):\n            idx = int(rng.randint(0, len(fitness)))\n            selected.append(idx)\n        best_idx = selected[0]\n        for idx in selected[1:]:\n            if fitness[idx] < fitness[best_idx]:\n                best_idx = idx\n        return best_idx\n\n    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\n        if self.controller is not None:\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\n\n        pop_size = self.pop_size\n        min_pop_size = max(4, int(0.2 * pop_size))\n        lb = self.lower_bounds\n        ub = self.upper_bounds\n        \n        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\n\n        # Latin Hypercube Sampling initialization for better initial coverage\n        initial_pop_size = pop_size\n        min_pop_size = max(4, int(0.2 * initial_pop_size))\n        pop = np.empty((pop_size, self.dim))\n        for j in range(self.dim):\n            sample_perm = rng.permutation(pop_size)\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                pop[i, j] = lb[j] + (sample_perm[i] + rng.rand()) / pop_size * (ub[j] - lb[j])\n        \n        # Evaluate initial population\n        evals = 0\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n        \n        for i in range(pop_size):\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                break\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\n            evals += 1\n\n        # Opposition-based learning: evaluate mirrored points and keep the best N\n        if evals + pop_size <= self.max_evals:\n            opp_pop = lb + ub - pop\n            opp_fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                    break\n                opp_fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, opp_pop[i])\n                evals += 1\n            all_pop = np.vstack([pop, opp_pop])\n            all_fit = np.concatenate([fitness, opp_fitness])\n            keep_order = np.argsort(all_fit)[:pop_size]\n            pop = all_pop[keep_order].copy()\n            fitness = all_fit[keep_order].copy()\n\n        # Track best\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\n        \n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\n\n        # ---- SHADE (Success-History based Adaptive DE) self.max_evals)\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\n            \n            done = (evals >= return float(problem(x))\n\n\nclass DEOptimizer:\n    \"\"\"Differential Evolution optimizer with DQN controller support.\n    \n    The controller outputs F and CR parameters dynamically during Tuple\nimport numpy as np\nimport os\nimport sys\n\n# Import SEED from config if available\n_config_seed = None\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\nfrom config import SEED as _config_seed\n\n\ndef evaluate_problem(problem, x):\n    \"\"\"Evaluate problem, handling both MetaBox (problem.func) and cocoex (problem(x)) styles.\"\"\"\n    if not isinstance(x, np.ndarray):\n        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)\n    \n    if hasattr(problem, 'func'):\n        # MetaBox style: use problem.func(x), which REQUIRES 2D input [NP, dim]\n        if x.ndim == 1:\n            x = x.reshape(1, -1)\n        result = problem.func(x)\n        return float(np.mean(np.array(result).flatten()))\n    else:\n        # cocoex style: problem(x)\n        return float(problem(x))\n\n\nclass DEOptimizer:\n    \"\"\"Differential Evolution optimizer with DQN controller support.\n    \n    The controller outputs F and CR parameters dynamically during optimization.\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, dim: int, lower_bounds, upper_bounds, max_evals: int,\n                 params: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None, controller=None, recorder=None,\n                 swarm_size: int = None):\n        self.dim = int(dim)\n        if lower_bounds is not None:\n            self.lower_bounds = np.asarray(lower_bounds, dtype=float)\n            if self.lower_bounds.ndim == 0:\n                self.lower_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.lower_bounds))\n        else:\n            self.lower_bounds = -5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n        \n        if upper_bounds is not None:\n            self.upper_bounds = np.asarray(upper_bounds, dtype=float)\n            if self.upper_bounds.ndim == 0:\n                self.upper_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.upper_bounds))\n        else:\n            self.upper_bounds = 5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n        \n        self.max_evals = int(max_evals)\n        self.params = dict(params) if params is not None else {}\n        self.controller = controller\n        self.recorder = recorder\n        self.stats = {}\n        self.pop_size = swarm_size if swarm_size is not None else max(10, 5 * self.dim)\n        self.F = float(self.params.get('F', 0.5))\n        self.CR = float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n\n    def _tournament_select(self, fitness, tournsize, rng):\n        \"\"\"Tournament selection with tournsize.\"\"\"\n        selected = []\n        for _ in range(tournsize):\n            idx = int(rng.randint(0, len(fitness)))\n            selected.append(idx)\n        best_idx = selected[0]\n        for idx in selected[1:]:\n            if fitness[idx] < fitness[best_idx]:\n                best_idx = idx\n        return best_idx\n\n    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\n        if self.controller is not None:\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\n\n        pop_size = self.pop_size\n        min_pop_size = max(4, int(0.2 * pop_size))\n        lb = self.lower_bounds\n        ub = self.upper_bounds\n        \n        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\n\n        # Latin Hypercube Sampling initialization for better initial coverage\n        initial_pop_size = pop_size\n        min_pop_size = max(4, int(0.2 * initial_pop_size))\n        pop = np.empty((pop_size, self.dim))\n        for j in range(self.dim):\n            sample_perm = rng.permutation(pop_size)\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                pop[i, j] = lb[j] + (sample_perm[i] + rng.rand()) / pop_size * (ub[j] - lb[j])\n        \n        # Evaluate initial population\n        evals = 0\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n        \n        for i in range(pop_size):\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                break\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\n            evals += 1\n\n        # Opposition-based learning: evaluate mirrored points and keep the
